I don't have a plan to actually do anything. I just... I don't know. Sometimes I just think it would be easier if I didn't wake up. Or if I could just... disappear. That way I wouldn't mess anything else up.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Um... a while, I guess. Maybe... a year and two months? It kind of started after I graduated high school... right around when I began my college classes and started working full-time at the clinic.\n\nDoctor: Do you still find pleasure in activities that you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nI used to do things to clear my head after class... like jogging, or knitting. Sometimes cooking or reading. But now... I don't know. I just get home and sit on my bed in my scrubs. I just can't really make myself care about any of it anymore.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Um... like I just said... no. I just don't care about any of it anymore.\n\nDoctor: Are there any small things that happen during the day that still bring you a little bit of happiness?\n\nPatient: Not really... no. \n\nI mean, I don't know what else to say.
